The sorting happens in javascript, and can take up to a full minute depending on how beefy your computer is.

If you have trouble accessing Fetlife directly from the list (error 422 something or the other) either reload the page to reset your referrer, or else set up your browser to no longer forward referrer information to websites (this is ideal, as it will serve your privacy more broadly than just on Fetlife).
